NOTE|info|venue_policy|MATCH stadium/city = the home club's documented ground for that season per the Wikipedia season articles' stadium/location tables (second index; RSSSF carries no venues), transcribed to audit/ledger/fra-venues.txt (94 entries = 20+20+18+18+18) and NFKD-folded to ASCII.
